add_namespace = ac_sq
country_event = {
	id = ac_sq.101
	title = ac_sq.101.t
	desc = {
		text = ac_sq.101.d_base
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_view_1_base
		}
	}
	picture = GFX_ac_sq_blk_scene_1
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = BLK original_tag = BLK country_exists = BLK ac_sq_active = yes has_country_flag = ac_sq_dispatch_pending check_variable = { ac_sq_chapter = 0 } check_variable = { ac_sq_pending_chapter = 1 } NOT = { date < 1991.1.1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_chapter_1 } NOT = { date > 1994.12.31 }
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = BLK original_tag = BLK country_exists = BLK ac_sq_active = yes has_country_flag = ac_sq_dispatch_pending check_variable = { ac_sq_chapter = 0 } check_variable = { ac_sq_pending_chapter = 1 } NOT = { date < 1991.1.1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_chapter_1 } NOT = { date > 1994.12.31 } NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_delivered_1 }
			}
			set_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_delivered_1
			if = {
				limit = {
					always = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_view_1_base
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ac_sq.101.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		trigger = {
			tag = BLK original_tag = BLK country_exists = BLK ac_sq_active = yes has_country_flag = ac_sq_dispatch_pending check_variable = { ac_sq_chapter = 0 } check_variable = { ac_sq_pending_chapter = 1 } NOT = { date < 1991.1.1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_chapter_1 } NOT = { date > 1994.12.31 } has_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_delivered_1
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = ac_sq.101.a.reply
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = BLK original_tag = BLK country_exists = BLK ac_sq_active = yes has_country_flag = ac_sq_dispatch_pending check_variable = { ac_sq_chapter = 0 } check_variable = { ac_sq_pending_chapter = 1 } NOT = { date < 1991.1.1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_chapter_1 } NOT = { date > 1994.12.31 } has_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_delivered_1
			}
			set_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_chapter_1
			set_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_learn_habits
			set_country_flag = ac_sq_has_read
			set_variable = { ac_sq_chapter = 1 }
			add_to_variable = { ac_sq_cohesion = 5 }
			add_to_variable = { ac_sq_credibility = -1 }
			clamp_variable = { var = ac_sq_cohesion min = 0 max = 100 }
			clamp_variable = { var = ac_sq_credibility min = 0 max = 100 }
			clr_country_flag = ac_sq_dispatch_pending
			set_variable = { ac_sq_pending_chapter = 0 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = ac_sq_cooldown days = 30 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ac_sq.101.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		trigger = {
			tag = BLK original_tag = BLK country_exists = BLK ac_sq_active = yes has_country_flag = ac_sq_dispatch_pending check_variable = { ac_sq_chapter = 0 } check_variable = { ac_sq_pending_chapter = 1 } NOT = { date < 1991.1.1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_chapter_1 } NOT = { date > 1994.12.31 } has_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_delivered_1
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = ac_sq.101.b.reply
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = BLK original_tag = BLK country_exists = BLK ac_sq_active = yes has_country_flag = ac_sq_dispatch_pending check_variable = { ac_sq_chapter = 0 } check_variable = { ac_sq_pending_chapter = 1 } NOT = { date < 1991.1.1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_chapter_1 } NOT = { date > 1994.12.31 } has_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_delivered_1
			}
			set_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_chapter_1
			set_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_ask_records
			set_country_flag = ac_sq_has_read
			set_variable = { ac_sq_chapter = 1 }
			add_to_variable = { ac_sq_cohesion = -1 }
			add_to_variable = { ac_sq_credibility = 5 }
			clamp_variable = { var = ac_sq_cohesion min = 0 max = 100 }
			clamp_variable = { var = ac_sq_credibility min = 0 max = 100 }
			clr_country_flag = ac_sq_dispatch_pending
			set_variable = { ac_sq_pending_chapter = 0 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = ac_sq_cooldown days = 30 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ac_sq_expired_close
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				tag = BLK original_tag = BLK country_exists = BLK ac_sq_active = yes has_country_flag = ac_sq_dispatch_pending check_variable = { ac_sq_chapter = 0 } check_variable = { ac_sq_pending_chapter = 1 } NOT = { date < 1991.1.1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_chapter_1 } NOT = { date > 1994.12.31 } has_country_flag = ac_sq_blk_delivered_1
			}
		}
	}
}

	id = ac_sq.619
	title = ac_sq.619.t
	desc = {

		trigger = {

		}

	}

		if = {
			limit = {

			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					always = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = ac_sq_eva_view_19_base
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ac_sq.619.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		trigger = {
			tag = EVA original_tag = EVA country_exists = EVA ac_sq_active = yes has_country_flag = ac_sq_dispatch_pending check_variable = { ac_sq_chapter = 18 } check_variable = { ac_sq_pending_chapter = 19 } NOT = { date < 2004.11.22 } NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_sq_eva_chapter_19 } has_country_flag = ac_sq_eva_delivered_19
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = ac_sq.619.a.reply
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = EVA original_tag = EVA country_exists = EVA ac_sq_active = yes has_country_flag = ac_sq_dispatch_pending check_variable = { ac_sq_chapter = 18 } check_variable = { ac_sq_pending_chapter = 19 } NOT = { date < 2004.11.22 } NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_sq_eva_chapter_19 } has_country_flag = ac_sq_eva_delivered_19
			}
			set_country_flag = ac_sq_eva_chapter_19
			set_country_flag = ac_sq_eva_eva_nadia_teaches
			set_country_flag = ac_sq_has_read
			set_variable = { ac_sq_chapter = 19 }
			add_to_variable = { ac_sq_cohesion = 4 }
			add_to_variable = { ac_sq_credibility = 3 }
			clamp_variable = { var = ac_sq_cohesion min = 0 max = 100 }
			clamp_variable = { var = ac_sq_credibility min = 0 max = 100 }
			clr_country_flag = ac_sq_dispatch_pending
			set_variable = { ac_sq_pending_chapter = 0 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = ac_sq_cooldown days = 30 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ac_sq.619.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		trigger = {
			tag = EVA original_tag = EVA country_exists = EVA ac_sq_active = yes has_country_flag = ac_sq_dispatch_pending check_variable = { ac_sq_chapter = 18 } check_variable = { ac_sq_pending_chapter = 19 } NOT = { date < 2004.11.22 } NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_sq_eva_chapter_19 } has_country_flag = ac_sq_eva_delivered_19

			set_country_flag = ac_sq_has_read

			add_to_variable = { ac_sq_credibility = -2 }
			clamp_variable = { var = ac_sq_cohesion min = 0 max = 100 }
			clamp_variable = { var = ac_sq_credibility min = 0 max = 100 }

			set_variable = { ac_sq_pending_chapter = 0 }

		}
